# WebexOne 2026 Speakers and Sessions Knowledge Base

This knowledge base supports attendee questions about WebexOne 2026 speakers and sessions. It includes speaker profiles, session details, speaker appearances, and location information from the supplied WebexOne app exports.

# Agent Response Guidelines

Use the session title, date, time, and location exactly as listed.

When a session title appears more than once, provide every listed delivery unless the attendee specifies a date or time.

Use the speaker profile for title, company, category, and biography. Use the session listing to answer when and where a speaker is appearing.

Do not invent a speaker biography, company, or session detail when it is not included in this document.

For room directions or last-minute changes, direct attendees to the WebexOne app, which has the current agenda and venue maps.

The Expo Theater and Quick Takes Theater are located on Level 3 in the Innovation & Solutions Showcase on Floor 3. This is also called the Expo Hall or Showcase.

# Speaker Directory

## Abdalnafie Sibaie

Title and company: AI Specialist, Team Lead, CarShield

Speaker category: CarShield, Customer/Partner

Biography: Sibaie is the AI Specialist and Team Lead of the AI Team at CarShield, where he leads the design and deployment of AI across the contact center and beyond. His team builds production voice AI agents, AI-powered quality assurance pipelines, agentic applications, and workflow automations spanning Cisco Webex Contact Center, Azure AI Foundry, and modern voice AI platforms.
His work focuses on moving AI out of the demo stage and into real workflows, from automated claims review to AI agents handling live customer conversations at scale.
Sibaie holds a degree in Computer Engineering from Missouri University of Science and Technology.

## Amey Parandekar

Title and company: Vice President of Product Management for Cisco Calling and on-premise UCM, Cisco

Speaker category: Cisco

Cisco speaker category: BREAKOUT SESSION SPEAKERS

Biography: Amey leads Product Management for Cisco Calling including cloud Calling, on-premise Unified Communications Manager (UCM) and Broadworks. Cisco calling is an easy to use, mobile integrated, enterprise grade, innovative calling phone system that is easy to setup and manage for IT admins, with best-in-class security & global availability.
He is passionate about innovation and evolving the calling experience from hearing, to seeing, to doing; empowering people to have a richer calling experience with Webex and leveraging cloud capabilities for the new era of hybrid work.
Prior to Cisco, Amey built Microsoft Teams calling and meetings from ground up and grew Teams adoption to millions of users. He also led Product management for Microsoft's Azure cloud IaaS/PaaS services across different verticals segments including sovereign clouds and government verticals. He has extensive experience working at large companies as well as startups, building innovative products and bringing them to market.
Amey brings more than 25 years of experience in areas such as product management, design, cloud engineering in productivity software at Microsoft Office, natural language processing, machine learning with search and NUI experiences, hardware integration for audio and video and analytics. He has developed 20 patents over his career.
Amey has a B.Tech from Indian Institute of Technology, Bombay and a Masters from North Carolina State University. He lives in Seattle with his family and enjoys outdoor activities such as hiking and kayaking in his free time.

## Amit Barave

Title and company: VP & GM, Webex Suite & AI, Cisco

Speaker category: Cisco Keynote

Cisco speaker category: KEYNOTE SPEAKERS

Biography: Amit Barave is Vice President and General Manager for Cisco's Webex Suite and AI, where he leads a multi-billion-dollar SaaS business delivering collaboration solutions to enterprises worldwide. His portfolio includes Webex messaging, meetings, calling, events, and customer experience platforms, all enhanced by AI capabilities.
Amit has shaped Webex's AI strategy and led the transformation of the Webex Suite to meet the demands of hybrid work. Under his leadership, the product team has advanced AI-powered collaboration while driving customer value and market leadership.
Throughout his career at Cisco, Amit has held leadership roles across product, engineering, and business functions. He launched industry-first products in TelePresence Cloud Services and IP-Radio communications, scaling them to market-leading positions. His track record includes sustained growth across large-scale product lines and successful business transformations.
Amit holds an MBA from the Haas School of Business at UC Berkeley and has been awarded 17 US patents in communications technologies. His technical expertise spans general management, product strategy, artificial intelligence, SaaS platforms, enterprise software, and go-to-market execution.

## Bobby McGonigle Jr

Title and company: Technical Marketing Engineer, Cisco

Speaker category: Cisco

Cisco speaker category: TRAINING SESSION SPEAKERS

Biography: I’m a Technical Marketing Engineer in Cisco’s Devices Business Unit, focusing on RoomOS products designed for integrators, including the Codec Pro G2 and Codec EQ. My interest in collaboration technology began during a university internship, where my first exposure to professional audiovisual systems centered on Cisco. I was hooked.
I’m now in my fifth year at Cisco, where I specialize in RoomOS xAPIs, macros, and room design. Before joining Cisco, I spent four years supporting RoomOS devices in education, an environment where complex technology needs to feel simple and natural for teachers. That experience still shapes my work today. I like to think of myself as a Cisco customer with a little extra access, helping make a product I already love even better.
One project I’m especially proud of is the Join Zoom macro. I originally created it at the university to make joining Zoom SIP meetings easier for my users, then shared it with the wider Cisco community. After joining Cisco, I had the opportunity to watch that idea evolve into an official product capability. The final implementation was not my code, but it solved the same problem and reflected the user experience I had set out to create. The macro has since been retired in favor of the current Join Zoom solution, but I’m proud that something I built for my users served the wider community and lived a full life.
That journey captures what I enjoy most about RoomOS. I want to help people discover what they can create with it. My goal as a speaker is to take people with little or no coding or xAPI experience and give them the confidence to interact with RoomOS devices like professionals.

## Jeetu Patel

Title and company: President & Chief Product Officer, Cisco

Speaker category: Cisco Keynote

Cisco speaker category: KEYNOTE SPEAKERS

Biography: Jeetu Patel is Cisco’s President and Chief Product Officer. He combines a bold vision, steeped in product design and development expertise, operational rigor, and innate market understanding to create high growth businesses. He is relentlessly focused on building world class products that solve Cisco customers’ biggest problems—bringing the power of the Cisco portfolio together to connect and protect every aspect of their organization in the era of AI. Previously he was Cisco’s Executive Vice President and General Manager of Security and Collaboration where he led the strategy and development for these businesses and held P&L responsibility for the multibillion-dollar portfolio. In this role, Jeetu led with his creative vision and intense focus on innovation and swift execution.
Together with his team, he transformed and positioned our Security and Collaboration portfolios for success and growth. In both areas, he reinvigorated organic innovation, championed key inorganic investments, and drove simplification across these portfolios with a fanatical focus on design and user experience. Prior to joining Cisco in 2020, Jeetu was the Chief Product Officer (CPO) and Chief Strategy Officer (CSO) at Box, a role he pioneered. He led the company’s product and platform strategy, setting the company’s long-term vision and roadmap for cloud content management in the enterprise. He transformed Box from a single product application to a multi-product platform used by 100K customers representing 69% of the Fortune 500. The discipline, quality standards, performance metrics, and stability Jeetu instilled fueled the platform’s growth – nearly quadrupling revenues to $700M+. Box’s growth scaled to reach over 60M users with over 50% of customers using multiple products.
He also created the Box Platform business unit where he led product strategy, marketing and developer relations – driving products from incubation stage to mature offerings. Before joining Box, Jeetu was General Manager and Chief Executive of EMC’s newly acquired Syncplicity business unit, a cloud service for Enterprise File Sync Sharing (EFSS) and collaboration. One of the first SaaS-based solutions offered by EMC, Jeetu spearheaded the company’s acquisition. He created a world class leadership team, secured some of the market’s largest customers and led the group to become one of the fastest growing EFSS companies in a highly competitive market. Other key roles at EMC included CMO for the Information Intelligence Group and Chief Strategy Officer, where he drove the organic and inorganic strategy for the division’s cloud and mobile growth. Previously, Jeetu was President of Doculabs, a research and advisory firm co-owned by Forrester Research. The firm focused on collaboration and content management across a range of industries including financial services, insurance, energy, manufacturing, and life sciences. He currently serves on the board of JLL, an American commercial real estate services company. Jeetu holds a B.S. in Information Decision Sciences from the University of Illinois, Chicago, and lives in the San Francisco Bay Area with his family.

Joseph is the architect behind AEO’s extensive Webex automation platform. His team has built 50+ custom bots and API integrations that have transformed store provisioning from a one-hour manual process into a five-minute, fully consistent workflow. These solutions connect Webex with Meraki, ServiceChannel, Jira, and internal store systems to deliver automated device provisioning, intelligent ticket enrichment, real-time alerting, and AI-assisted support. The same platform is now expanding into Webex Contact Center AI agents designed to reduce seasonal staffing needs and preserve institutional knowledge.
His work emphasizes practical scale: 4,572 devices under management, “Real Vision Rooms” combining Cisco Codec EQ, Pro Ceiling Microphones, and wireless cameras, and the integration of Cisco EQ into AEO’s Town Hall for accessible large-scale Webex Webinars. The results include dramatically faster deployments, higher operational consistency, and a measurable shift of support effort from repetitive tasks to higher-value problem solving.
At Webex One, Joseph shares a retail practitioner’s view of what full-suite Webex adoption looks like when automation, device strategy, and AI are treated as core operational capabilities rather than side projects.

### Speaking Appearances

AI Across Industries: What's Working, What's Stalled, and What's Next — October 7th, 2026 | 1:50 pm - 3:05 pm | Wisteria, Level 4

Biography: As the current Technical Leader for Cisco's TAC in Webex Calling Technology, Luis has been at the vanguard of enhancing global support capabilities and refining the Webex Calling experience for customers and engineers alike. At the heart of Luis's work is a passion for mentorship. By dedicating a significant portion of time to guide and nurture the skills of TAC engineers, Luis ensures that the next generation of technical support is prepared to meet the complex demands of modern collaboration solutions. This commitment to excellence extends beyond team development, as Luis actively contributes to the improvement of product serviceability.
Renowned for expertise in complex hybrid Collaboration implementations, Luis plays a pivotal role in assisting customers as they transition to cloud-based solutions. This expertise is underpinned by a ten-year mastery of the CCIE Collaboration Certification #40952, a testament to Luis's dedication and skill in the field.
Driven by an unwavering commitment to instigate positive change, Luis is instrumental in elevating the Webex Calling Offer. Through a focus on customer support and service enhancement, Luis not only addresses current needs but also paves the way for a more innovative and reliable future in cloud collaboration. #Teacher #Pioneer

Title and company: Assistant Vice President, Infrastructure Services, Americo

Biography: Richard is the Assistant Vice President of Infrastructure Services at Americo, providing strategic oversight for the company's enterprise technology infrastructure. With more than 30 years of experience in IT leadership, Richard plays a pivotal role

business objectives. introduced new technologies across the organization.

His presentations have addressed topics such as emerging IT trends, help desk operations, collaboration tools, and infrastructure resilience.
Born and raised on the island of Guam, Richard moved to the United States at 16. He and his wife of 16 years have a large blended family, with seven children, fifteen grandchildren, and one great-grandchild. He comes from a long line of military
service members and is a proud recipient of the Quilt of Valor, honoring his family's legacy of dedication and sacrifice.

## Tim Messenger

Title and company: Team Manager, Unified Communications - Contact Center, Community Health Network

Speaker category: Community Health Network, Customer/Partner

Biography: Tim Messenger has spent two decades supporting the telephony environment at Community Health Network. Prior to working in IT, Tim spent time doing low voltage cabling and phone system installation, as well as a few years working in Emergency Medicine before returning to the world of IT. Initially starting as a contractor at CHNw for the E911 location mapping project, then joining the Voice Services/UC Team as an analyst supporting Nortel, and eventually Cisco Call Manager and UCCX. In 2012, he accepted a position as a Contact Center analyst supporting the UCCE IP/IVR environment. Then assisted in the migration to UCCE CVP.
In 2017, a specific Contact Center Team was created, with Tim as the manager. During this time, he and his team assisted in many environment upgrades, support, and enhancements. In 2024, CHNw began the process of building the first WxCC contact centers, setting the stage for a full migration from UCCE. Tim and his team migrated over 120 contact centers and over 400 IVRs as well as deploying centralized scheduling and triage for Primary Care practices. During this they also worked with Cisco and Presidio to develop the first deployment of WxCC AI for a pilot program for a rescheduling agent integrated with the existing practice IVRs. After the pilot was completed, the UC Contact Center Team deployed the AI Rescheduling Agent to an additional 50 practices.
Currently, they are working on the next use cases covering New Appointment Scheduling, Registration, as well as Bill Pay/Payment Plans. Tim and his team continue to evolve to support the growing changes of the Contact Center world to help ensure the patients and care givers at Community Health Network have the best experience and care as possible.

This session examines the architecture, integration, and proactive management of these features, providing the technical foundation for high-availability hybrid voice deployments.

## Deploying Secure AI for UCCE and Private Cloud Environments

Date and time: October 5th, 2026 | 9:15 am - 10:15 am

Location: Iris, Level 4

Program type: Technical Training Class

Session summary: Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Topics: Integrations, AI, Customer Experience, Contact Center, Platform/Architecture, Control Hub

Speakers: Chandramouli Vaithiyanathan (Cisco), Rajiv Krishnan (Cisco)

Abstract: Organizations running on-premises UCCE and private cloud environments face a critical challenge: how to adopt generative AI without compromising security, data sovereignty, or control. This session provides a technical and strategic framework for deploying AI agents in high-security contact center environments while preserving the integrity of existing infrastructure.
We will explore four key areas. First, you'll learn how Customer Voice Portal (CVP) and existing call flows integrate with Cisco AI Studio, including the technical handoffs and orchestration between legacy logic and modern AI services.
Next, you'll learn how to address data sovereignty and security by defining clear boundaries between private cloud environments and externally processed workloads to help close the trust gap.
We'll then explore strategic AI orchestration and how to move beyond a single-bot model toward a multi-agent architecture. Topics include Silent Transfer, Announced Transfer and MCP handoffs, as well as how to establish boundaries for agent specialization.
Finally, we'll cover governance and advanced observability, including how to implement Cisco's AI Impact Assessment framework to manage fairness and safety while proactively monitoring for hallucinations, accuracy drift, and system prompt changes.
You'll leave with a clearer path for modernizing UCCE with secure, observable AI while maintaining control over infrastructure, data, and deployment-and a practical framework for bridging legacy reliability with autonomous customer experience.
CE credits: 2

## Unifying the Webex Platform with Webex Contact Center and Webex Calling

Date and time: October 5th, 2026 | 10:30 am - 12:00 pm

Location: Park View Ballroom B, Level 7

Program type: Technical Training Class

Session summary: Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Topics: Integrations, Customer Experience, Calling, Devices, Contact Center, Platform/Architecture

Speakers: Amy Ryan (Cisco), Carles Duz Palau (Cisco)

Abstract: This technical session explores the architecture and capabilities of a unified Webex platform. We will examine how Webex Contact Center and Webex Calling streamline infrastructure, simplify deployments, and power seamless customer experiences. We will begin with a brief overview of today’s Webex Contact Center Common Edge Services, followed by a quick look at future use cases that Webex Common Media Services will enable.
The core of the session will focus on the technical mechanics of Webex Contact Center Common Edge Services. We will dive deep into PSTN and routing options including Cloud Connected PSTN (CCP) and Bring Your Own PSTN (BYOPSTN). We'll cover the Edge Connect architecture to bring media to Webex using private link, as well as global deployment strategies with a spotlight on supporting operations in India.
Finally, we will cover key cross-platform integrations designed to optimize operations. You will learn about advanced reporting that captures Webex Calling call legs, Webex Calling device control, WebRTC enhancements, and Webex Messaging for agent-to-supervisor collaboration. We will conclude with roadmap highlights.
CE credits: 2

Along the way, we’ll look at how Cisco devices enable smarter workspace management, IoT, automation, sustainability, and better employee experiences. ## Beyond the AI Pilot: A Flight Plan for Trusted CX at Scale

Date and time: October 6th, 2026 | 8:00 am - 10:00 am

Location: Indigo, Level 4

Program type: Technical Training Class

Session summary: Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Topics: Troubleshooting, AI, Customer Experience, Contact Center, Platform/Architecture

Abstract: AI pilots are multiplying but trusted, measurable value at scale remains elusive. The challenge is not choosing another AI capability; it is creating an operating model that connects technology, people, data, governance, and outcomes across the customer journey.
In this intermediate session, contact center architects, administrators, and CX leaders will use the F.L.I.G.H.T. framework to move beyond experimentation: Find the right use case, Launch deliberately, Interact and tune, Govern and secure, Handoff with context, and Transform through measurable outcomes. Grounded in Webex Contact Center and informed by real deployments, end-to-end walkthroughs reveal the decisions that separate pilots from durable operations. This includes: where AI belongs, how it should be tuned and measured, when humans should enter the journey, and how trust is maintained as autonomy grows. Leave with practical principles for scaling AI without losing context, control, or sight of business value.

## Instructing the Agent: The Knowledge Layer Behind Trusted CX AI

Date and time: October 6th, 2026 | 5:25 pm - 5:45 pm

Location: Quick Takes Theater, Level 3

Program type: In-Person, Quick Takes

Topics: AI, Customer Experience

Speakers: James Hunt (eGain)

Abstract: Cisco has put real AI capabilities in the hands of CX leaders today, but what that capability has exposed is a problem sitting one layer down: the knowledge these systems run on is fragmented, inconsistent, and ungoverned.
An AI agent is only as reliable as what it has been given. Outdated policies and procedures, duplicate content, and conflicting instructions produce wrong answers to customers, failed automation, and compliance risk, eroding trust in the brand.
This session looks at how CX leaders build a trusted instruction layer for enterprise AI. Drawing on hyper-scale deployments, eGain will show how to move past traditional knowledge management to AI-ready infrastructure, so Cisco customers get accurate answers and actions every time.

## C.A.R.E.S.: A New Standard for Measuring Human + AI Contact Center Performance

Date and time: October 6th, 2026 | 5:50 pm - 6:10 pm

Location: Quick Takes Theater, Level 3

Program type: Quick Takes, In-Person

Session summary: One independent framework for measuring performance across all agents

Topics: AI, Customer Experience, Contact Center

Speakers: David Rennyson (SuccessKPI)

Abstract: Is a contained AI interaction really a successful interaction? And are humans always better than AI? As AI agents handle more customer conversations, contact centers need a common standard for measuring performance across the human + AI workforce.
This session introduces the C.A.R.E.S. framework: Compliance, Accuracy, Resolution, Effort and Sentiment. Learn how to compare human and AI performance by customer intent, determine where AI outperforms humans—and where it doesn’t—and identify opportunities to improve both.
See how SuccessKPI AI AutoQM and interaction analytics apply these measures at scale, uncover failures traditional metrics miss, and provide independent performance reporting and governance.

## AI-Powered Meetings: Build a Follow-Up Agent and Troubleshooting Assistant with Webex Model Context Protocol (MCP)

Abstract: Imagine an AI agent that turns your meeting transcript into a follow-up Webex space with action items, or takes a meeting number and hands you back a quality analysis in seconds. Webex's new Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers make this possible by giving AI agents direct access to meeting data, transcripts, and messaging. In this hands-on lab, you'll learn what MCP is, enable the servers on a Webex org, and build two workflows: one that auto-creates a follow-up space from a transcript, and one that analyzes meeting quality and generates an HTML troubleshooting report.

## The Deepfake Crisis In Real-time Communication

Date and time: October 7th, 2026 | 4:45 pm - 5:05 pm

Location: Quick Takes Theater, Level 3

Program type: In-Person, Quick Takes

Session summary: How to help defend against AI fraud and improve customer experience

Topics: Employee Experience, AI, Customer Experience, Contact Center

Abstract: Is your organization prepared to defend against AI attacks? With bots, attackers are now empowered to impersonate executives or genuine customers, infiltrate hiring processes, manipulate helpdesks, and exploit the trust placed in familiar faces and voices. Over 74% of organizations have been hit by a deepfake, and most didn’t know it at the time.
Join Pindrop’s SVP of Global Partner Sales Sumant Mauskar and Ellery Heiniemi, Enterprise Technology Executive at Ameriprise Financial to learn how deepfake attacks are trending, why traditional security is no longer effective, and how leading organizations like Ameriprise are working to better secure their interactions across every real-time communication channel: from the contact center, to internal phone systems, to virtual meeting rooms.

## Women in Technology Panel: The Next Era of Leadership

Date and time: October 8th, 2026 | 7:45 am - 8:30 am

Location: Palm Park A, Level 7

Program type: In-Person, Breakout

Session summary: Join women leaders for a candid, forward-looking conversation about the next era of leadership.

Topics: AI

Speakers: Anna Hukill (Cisco), Courtenay Godshall (Cisco), Jenna Leahy (Cisco), Nikki Parsons (Akamai Technologies)

Abstract: All attendees will receive priority seating at the keynote! Grab breakfast and join a future-focused conversation with inspiring women about leadership in the age of AI.
What do we keep, what do we leave behind, and what do we build next?
The world around us is changing. Technology is reshaping how work gets done. Expectations of work, careers, and leaders are evolving. Traditional hierarchies are giving way to new ways of collaborating and influencing. And qualities once considered “soft,” from empathy and curiosity to judgment and trust, are becoming increasingly essential.
All of it raises a bigger question: Does our definition of great leadership need to change, too?
For decades, conversations about women and leadership have often focused on how women can succeed within existing systems. But as those systems evolve, we have an opportunity to ask something different: What if we helped shape what comes next?
Join women leaders from technology, business, and the nonprofit sector for a candid, forward-looking conversation about the next era of leadership. We'll explore what great leadership will require in a world transformed by AI and other forces of change; which traditional expectations of leaders are worth carrying forward and which may be holding us back; and how we can build a model of leadership that is more human, sustainable, and inclusive by design.
The future of leadership isn't predetermined. We have an opportunity to shape it now.

## Considerations For Designing Rooms with AVoIP

Date and time: October 8th, 2026 | 12:45 pm - 1:45 pm

Location: Park View Ballroom A, Level 7

Program type: Technical Training Class

Session summary: Technical Training Class: Intermediate

Topics: Troubleshooting, Employee Experience, Suite, Calling, Devices, Platform/Architecture, Control Hub

Speakers: Anna Bieber (Cisco), Astrid Kongelf Jensen (Cisco)

Abstract: An advanced room used to mean a camera, a handful of microphones, and speakers covering the space. Today those rooms are built from IP peripherals, which opens up experiences that weren't practical before. This session is about what you can build as their number grows, and the choices worth making early so the room still works the way you intended years later.
We'll start with the hardware: the full family of Cisco IP peripherals, how they differ from their non-IP counterparts, and which ones fit rooms of different size and shape. There may also be something new to see.
Good design is only half of it. The rest is the experience of building and living with the room, so we'll cover how setup and calibration are improving, how we're making these systems easier to manage and troubleshoot, and how a complex room stays predictable long after installation.
By the end you'll know what an advanced room can do, how to design for it, and what setup and ongoing management actually look like. We're the product managers for these features, so bring your questions.
